!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF0_2656
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = time point in exercise: T0 (baseline)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = Sex: female
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF0_2752
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = peripheral blood mononuclear cells
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: peripheral blood mononuclear cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = disease state: healthy control
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = time point in exercise: T0 (baseline)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = Sex: female
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF0_2944
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF1_2717
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF1_2929
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF2_2682
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF2_2790
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HF2_5264
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CF0_2797
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CF0_2701
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CF1_2702
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CF1_2798
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CF2_2655
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: peripheral blood mononuclear cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = disease state: ME/CFS patient
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = time point in exercise: T2 (recovery)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = Sex: female
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CF2_2775
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CF2_2967
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HM0_2794
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HM1_42
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HM1_2819
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = HM2_2688
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CM0_04
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CM0_44
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CM1_26
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CM2_12
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group

!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = peripheral blood mononuclear cells
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: peripheral blood mononuclear cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = disease state: ME/CFS patients
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = time point in exercise: T2 (recovery)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = Sex: male
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Total RNA was extracted using RNAzol (Molecular Research Center) according to the manufacturer’s instructions. RNA quality was assessed using Agilent TapeStation 4200 (Agilent Technologies). All RNA samples had an RNA Integrity number (RIN) above seven.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = The libraries were generated using Illumina TruSeq Stranded Total RNA Library Prep Kit
!Sample_description = CM2_37
!Sample_data_processing = Raw reads were mapped to the reference human genome (GRCh38, release 86) using GSNAP, HISAT2, and STAR software.
!Sample_data_processing = For the analysis of differentially expressed known genes, reads for each gene aligned by GSNAP and HISAT2 were counted using HTSeq software. Alignment by STAR was run with the option “quantMode TranscriptomeSAM” that allowed for the counting of reads aligned to each gene.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw counts from HTSeq and STAR were imported into Bioconductor/R package DESeq2, normalized, and tested for differential gene expression. We used the Bioconductor/R package DEXSeq to count individual exons and compare expression of exons. This analysis was done separately for the files produced by each aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Only genes with raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group were used for the differential gene expression analysis
!Sample_data_processing = For further analysis, we selected genes and exons that were differentially expressed based on one of the following two criteria: (a) either a fold change (FC) > |1.5| and false discovery rate (FDR) < 0.10 in one out of the three aligners and FC > |1.4| and FDR < 0.15 in the remaining two aligners, OR (b) a FC > |1.5| and FDR < 0.10 in two out of three aligners.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: GRCh38, release 86
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: Matrix table with raw gene counts for every gene and every sample, where only genes that had raw counts equal or more than 50 in all samples in at least one group
